Pseudo:
def lse_score_x(x, beta):
  n = len(x)+1; zval = _softmax_from_logrefs(x) # a sum=1
  c=fast_conv(a)
  cm=c.max()
  s=beta*(c-cm)
  # s max 0 maybe; subtract 0 for exp
  exps=np.exp(s)  # since s<=0, stable; but due rounding maybe >1e-12, clip
  exps = np.clip(exps, 0, None)
  sumexp = exps.sum()
  if sumexp==0? no at least 1.
  L = cm + np.log(sumexp)/beta
  F = 2.0*n*L
  p=exps/sumexp
  out = lse_crosscorr(a,p)
  gradL=2.0*out
  gradF=2.0*n*gradL
  D = float(np.dot(a, gradF))
  gradx = a[:-1]*(gradF[:-1]-D)
  return F, gradx

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size; L=_next_pow2(2*n-1); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]

def _conv_fast(a, L=None): maybe no.

def _crosscorr_p_a(a,p):
    n=a.size; L=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ra=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); ra[:n]=a[::-1]
    pp=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); pp[:2*n-1]=p
    r=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ra,L)*np.fft.rfft(pp,L), L)
    return r[1:n]

Need ensure p length 2*n-1. For n=1, L? 3n-2=1 OK. r[1:n] empty? n=1, r[1:1] empty; grad? If n=1, objective c=[a0^2], score=2. But n small not used. Could handle separately.

Pseudo:
def _obj_log(x):
    n=_OPT_N; idx=_OPT_IDX; ref=_OPT_REF; mode=_OPT_MODE
    # build a
    if x.size:
        mx = max(0.0, float(np.max(x)))
    else: mx=0.0
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx] = np.exp(x - mx)
    a[ref] = np.exp(-mx)
    ss = a.sum()
    if ss <= 0: return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x))
    a /= ss
    if mode == 0:
        F, gradF = _lse_score_grad_an(a, _OPT_BETA)
    else:
        F, gradF = _top_quadratic_score_grad_an(a, _OPT_P)
    D = float(np.dot(a, gradF))
    g = a[idx] * (gradF[idx] - D)
    return F, g

Need if x has NaN? L-BFGS should not. If exp underflow all zeros? a[ref]=exp(-mx); if mx huge, exp(-mx)=0, others exp(x-mx) some 1, sum>0. If x all -inf? not.

Top objective:
def _top_quadratic_score_grad_an(a,p):
    n=a.size
    c=_conv_full(a)
    P=float(np.dot(p,c))
    # p length 2n-1 sum 1
    out=_crosscorr_p_a(a,p) # out_i =∑a_j p_{i+j}
    gradF = 4.0*n*out
    return 2.0*n*P, gradF
But c and out both require FFT. out can be computed maybe from A and p; c needed only if mode? For top, need P. We can compute P via dot after c. Maybe c via FFT. Good.

LSE objective:
def _lse_score_grad_an(a,beta):
    n=a.size; c=_conv_full(a); cm=max(c)
    # if beta <=0? fallback top? assume.
    scaled = beta*(c-cm)
    # To avoid overflow if scaled positive due fp, clip to 700? But c-cm <=0 theoretically; set scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp=exps.sum()
    if sumexp <=0: # all underflow? At least one zero? if c all -inf? impossible. set p uniform?
       p=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1))
    else: p=exps/sumexp
    L=cm + np.log(sumexp)/beta
    out=_crosscorr_p_a(a,p)
    F=2.0*n*L
    gradF=4.0*n*out
    return F, gradF

Check gradient factor: L = cm + log(∑exp(β(c-cm)))/β.

def exact_score_sequence(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum(); if S<0.01 return inf; c=_conv_full(a); return 2n*max(c)/S^2.

def top_weights_from_c(c, K, alpha, beta_smooth=0.0):
   L=c.size; p=zeros
   # top uniform
   idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
   w=1.0/K
   # maybe add softmax over all lags: q=exp(beta*(c-cm))/sum; p=alpha*w/(K?) (w sum 1) + (1-alpha)q.
   if alpha<1:
      cm=c.max(); s=beta_smooth*(c-cm); s = min(s,0); q=exp(s)/sum; p = alpha*(idx uniform) + (1-alpha)*q
   return p

Now coordinate/interior updates:

Helper `_pow2(m)`.
`_conv_full(a)`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n = a.size
    L = _pow2(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
```

`_crosscorr_p_a(a,p)`:
```
def _crosscorr_p_a(a,p):
    n=a.size
    L=_pow2(3*n-2)
    ra=np.zeros(L); ra[:n]=a[::-1]
    pp=np.zeros(L); pp[:2*n-1]=p
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ra,L)*np.fft.rfft(pp,L), L)[1:n]
```
If n=1, L=1, ra[:1]=a[0], pp[:1]=p[0], irfft length1, [1:1] empty. But grad length n? For n=1 should return [2a0?]. Derivative c0=a0^2, derivative 2a0. Our formula out_i = p_i a_i? For n=1, i=0: out= p0 a0. GradL=2out=2a0. We can special-case:

def _obj_log(x):
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout
    n=_OPT_N; idx=_OPT_IDX; ref=_OPT_REF; mode=_OPT_MODE
    if x.size:
       mx=float(np.max(x))
       if mx < 0.0: mx=0.0
    else: mx=0.0
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx]=np.exp(x-mx)
    a[ref]=math.exp(-mx) if -mx > -700 else 0.0? math.exp(-mx) if mx<700 else 0.0
    ss=a.sum()
    if ss <= 1e-300:
       # fallback uniform
       a.fill(1.0); a/=n
       ss=1.0
    else:
       a/=ss
    if mode == 0:
       F, gradF = _lse_score_grad_an(a, _OPT_BETA)
    else:
       F, gradF = _top_quad_score_grad_an(a, _OPT_P)
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
       raise _OptTimeout
    if F < _OBJ_BEST_VAL:
       _OBJ_BEST_VAL = F
       _OBJ_BEST_X = x.copy()
    D = float(np.dot(a, gradF))
    g = a[idx]*(gradF[idx]-D)
    # Replace NaN? If F inf return 1e100 zeros
    if not np.isfinite(F):
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(F), g
```

def make_top_weights(c, K=100, alpha=0.7, beta=0.0):
   L=c.size; idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]; w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/K
   if alpha>=1: return w
   cm=c.max(); s=beta*(c-cm); s=np.minimum(s,0); q=np.exp(s); ss=q.sum(); if ss<=0: q=np.full(L,1/L); else q/=ss
   p=alpha*w + (1-alpha)*q
   p /= p.sum()
   return p
```

```
def resample_array(a, n): # top-level
  old=np.asarray(a,float); m=old.size
  if m==n: return old.copy()
  x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
  b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
  S=b.sum(); if S<... b[:]=1
  return b/S

Lowpass:
```
def lowpass_parent(a, keep_frac=0.2):
   n=len(a); L=_pow2(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   kmax=int(keep_frac*n)
   A[kmax+1:]=0
   b=np.fft.irfft(A,L)[:n]
   b=np.clip(b,0,1000); normalize.
```

def coordinate_peak_descent(a0, n_sweeps=30, max_coords=300, n_peaks=4, factors=(0.0,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.25,1.6), max_time=None, time_start=None):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a0,float),0,1000).copy()
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return a
    a=a/S
    n=a.size
    c=_conv_full(a); S=1.0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=2*n*c.max()
    current_sc=best_sc; current_S=1.0
    if max_time is None: max_time=np.inf
    if time_start is None: time_start=time.monotonic()
    for sweep in range(n_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic()-time_start > max_time: break
        # recompute c each sweep to reduce error
        c=_conv_full(a); current_S=float(a.sum()); current_sc=2*n*max(0,c.max())/(current_S*current_S)
        if current_sc<best_sc: best_sc=current_sc; best_a=a.copy()
        if current_S <0.2 or current_S>5:
            a=a/current_S; c=_conv_full(a); current_S=1; current_sc=2*n*c.max()
            if current_sc<best_sc: update
        # prefix/suffix
        Lc=c.size
        prefix=np.maximum.accumulate(np.maximum(c,0.0))
        prefix_full=np.empty(Lc+1); prefix_full[0]=0; prefix_full[1:]=prefix
        suff_rev=np.maximum.accumulate(np.maximum(c[::-1],0.0))
        suffix=np.empty(Lc+1); suffix[-1]=0; suffix[:Lc]=suff_rev[:Lc][::-1]? Let's do simpler:
        suffix=np.zeros(Lc+1); suffix[:Lc-1:-1]? Hard.
```

Mirror LSE implementation:
```
def mirror_lse(a0, n_iters=2000, beta=1000000.0, max_time=120.0):
   a=norm; best; current_sc
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(n_iters):
      if time.now > ... break
      c = _conv_full(a)
      cm=c.max(); scaled=min(beta*(c-cm),0); exps; p
      out = _crosscorr_p_a(a,p)
      h = 4.0*n*out  # positive
      if h.max()<=0: break
      step_base = 1.0/(h.max()+1e-300)
      best_sc_it=current_sc; best_a_it=a
      for factor in [0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]:
         eta=factor*step_base
         loga=np.log(a+1e-300) - eta*h
         loga -= loga.max()
         an=np.exp(loga); an/=an.sum()
         sc=score_unnorm(an)
         if sc<best_sc_it: update
      if best_sc_it < current_sc - 1e-12:
          a=best_a_it; current_sc=best_sc_it; no_improve=0; update best
      else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve>100: break
   return best_a
```
This uses exact score for candidates (FFT) each 4 candidates + one c for h => 5 conv per iter. Could be heavy but okay for 500. Set factors maybe 3. We can reduce iters if time. Use `max_time` 300. If beta too high, h one-hot; okay. Could adapt beta increasing? Start 5e5, maybe later 2e6. Use schedule outside.

Could use current c to compute p; h. For candidate score, use exact `score_unnorm` (conv). Good.

Now, let's reason correctness of gradient factors and objective for log/direct. Direct `_obj_direct`:
```
a = clip(x); S=a.sum(); an=a/S; F,gradF = score_grad_norm(an mode beta p)
D = dot(an, gradF)
g = (gradF - D) / S
```
If mode LSE, F=2nL(an), gradF derivative wrt an of 2nL (not including scaling). F(y)=F(an). Since L degree 2 and F Norm? Let's test with L=∑a^2? F=∑a^2 for S=1? For direct unnormalized, F(y)=∑(y/S)^2? But our F_norm uses L(a) without dividing by S^2? Wait! In normalized problem, a is already constrained sum=1. If we define F_norm(a)=2n L(a) with a sum1. For unnormalized y, the scale-invariant extension should be 2n L(y)/S^2, not 2n L(y/S)? But L(y/S)=L(y)/S^2 (because L degree2). Same. D=an·gradF_norm =? gradF_norm = ∂(2nL)/∂an. For L=(∑a)^2, an sum1, L=1, gradF=2n? D=2n? F=2n? direct gradient=(gradF-D)/S =0. Good.
Derive direct gradient with extension F(y)=2n L(y)/S^2. ∂ = 2n( gradL_y/S^2 - 2L(y)/S^3 *1). At y=S an, gradL_y=S gradF_norm/S? Let's compute: F_norm_a=2n gradL_a. L(y)=S^2 L_a, gradL_y=S*gradL_a. Gradient =2n( S gradL_a/S^2 - 2 S^2 L_a/S^3 *1) =2n(gradL_a/S - 2 L_a/S) = (2n gradL_a - 4n L_a)/S. gradF_norm=2n gradL_a. D=an·gradF_norm. For L degree2, D=2n*2 L_a? Wait Euler: an·gradL_a=2L_a. So D=2n*2L_a=4nL_a. Thus gradient=(gradF_norm - D)/S. Good. For any quadratic L (convolution quadratic) degree2, holds. Good.
For LSE L is not strictly quadratic but homogeneous? LSE(logsumexp βc) is not homogeneous degree2 except β→∞? L(c) for c homogeneous: if scale a by λ, c by λ^2, LSE(βλ^2 c) not scale λ^2 generally. But our F_norm for LSE is 2n LSE(β c(a)) with a sum1. To extend scale-invariantly, we should use F(y)=2n LSE(β c(y/S)) = 2n LSE(β c(y)/S^2). The extension gradient is not gradF-D with D Euler because LSE not homogeneous. Our direct gradient formula (gradF-D) comes from assuming extension F(y)=F_norm(y/S) where F_norm(a)=2n LSE(β c(a)) and we use a=y/S. The derivative of F(y)=2nLSE(β c(y/S)): a=y/S. gradF_a = derivative wrt a. ∂a_i/∂y_j = δ_ij/S - y_i/S^2. Then gradient = gradF_j/S - (1/S^2)∑ gradF_i y_i = (gradF_j - D_a/S? wait ∑ grad_i y_i = S D_a, /S^2 = D_a/S) => (gradF_j/S - D_a/S) = (gradF_j - D_a)/S. This does NOT require homogeneity. Good. F(y)=F_norm(a) with a normalized by sum; it is scale invariant by construction (because F_norm ignores sum? Actually F_norm(a) defined for any a? It uses LSE c(a), which depends on scale. But F(y)=F_norm(y/S) explicitly normalizes before computing c. So extension is scale invariant. Our gradient formula is correct for F(y)=F_norm(y/S) for arbitrary F_norm (not homogeneous). Nice. Therefore direct formula with gradF from F_norm at normalized an and D=an·gradF is exact. Good.
Log variables: x maps to a normalized exactly; gradient formula a_j(gradF_j-D) for ratios correct for arbitrary F_norm. Good.

Now, does F_norm for LSE at an normalized equal 2n LSE(β c(an)). It is scale invariant extension through normalization. Good.

For top p, F_norm=2n∑p c(a) (quadratic). Same.

Detailed code for helpers:

```
def _pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=0? use 1.

`_conv_full`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _pow2(2*n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
If a contains complex? We only pass float.

`_crosscorr_p_a`:
```
def _crosscorr_p_a(a,p):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if n==1:
        return np.array([float(p[0])*float(a[0])])
    L=_pow2(3*n-2)
    ra=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    ra[:n]=a[::-1]
    pp=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    pl=int(p.size)
    pp[:pl]=p[:pl]
    out=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ra,L)*np.fft.rfft(pp,L), L)
    return out[1:n]
```
If p length shorter? It is full. If longer, pl min.

`_score_grad_norm`:
```
def _score_grad_norm(a, mode, beta, p):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n == 0: return np.inf, np.zeros(0)
    if n == 1:
       val=float(a[0])
       c0=val*val
       # F_norm? For mode no matter, c0.
       # gradF = derivative of 2n c0 = 4 val
       return float(2.0*1.0*c0), np.array([4.0*val])
    c=_conv_full(a)
    if mode == 0:
       cm=float(np.max(c))
       if beta <= 0:
          p = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
          L = cm
       else:
          scaled = beta * (c - cm)
          np.minimum(scaled, 0.0, out=scaled)
          exps = np.exp(scaled)
          s = float(exps.sum())
          if s <= 0.0:
             p = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
             L = cm
          else:
             p = exps / s
             L = cm + math.log(s) / beta
       out = _crosscorr_p_a(a, p)
       F = 2.0 * n * L
       gradF = 4.0 * n * out
       return float(F), gradF
    else:
       if p is None:
          p = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
       P = float(np.dot(p, c))
       out = _crosscorr_p_a(a, p)
       F = 2.0 * n * P
       gradF = 4.0 * n * out
       return float(F), gradF
```
If beta tiny? use uniform. Good. `np.minimum` with out works if scaled float. For p length, c may have negative; scaled for negative large underflow. Good.

`_obj_direct`:
```
def _obj_direct(x):
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout
    n=_OPT_N
    a=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
       a = a[:n] if a.size>n else np.pad(a,(0,n-a.size))
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
       a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1,n)
       S=1.0
    an=a/S
    F, gradF = _score_grad_norm(an, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P)
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
       raise _OptTimeout
    if not np.isfinite(F):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if F < _OBJ_BEST_VAL:
       _OBJ_BEST_VAL = F
       _OBJ_BEST_X = a.copy()  # store normalized? For direct, x same; should store x (original) maybe. If clipped/normalized? We can store a (which equals clip x normalized? Actually x unnormalized, F from an. For recovery, a clipped sum maybe S; objective canonical y could be a (clipped) not normalized. Direct recovery normalize. Use a.copy() okay.
    D = float(np.dot(an, gradF))
    g = (gradF - D) / S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(F), g
```
If S very large, gradient /S small. But starting normalized, okay. `a.copy()` stores clipped x, not normalized; recovery normalizes. Good.

But `_OBJ_BEST_X` is global and used for log too; for direct stored length n. In run_log, `_OBJ_BEST_X` initialized x0 length n-1 and objective log stores length n-1. Good.

`_obj_log`:
```
def _obj_log(x):
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
       raise _OptTimeout
    n=_OPT_N; idx=_OPT_IDX; ref=_OPT_REF
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size:
       mx=float(np.max(x))
    else: mx=0.0
    if mx < 0.0: mx=0.0
    if x.size:
       a[idx]=np.exp(x - mx)
    if mx < 700.0:
       a[ref]=math.exp(-mx)
    else:
       a[ref]=0.0
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
       a.fill(1.0)
       a/=float(n)
       S=1.0
    else:
       a/=S
    F, gradF = _score_grad_norm(a, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P)
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE: raise _OptTimeout
    if not np.isfinite(F):
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if F < _OBJ_BEST_VAL:
       _OBJ_BEST_VAL=F
       _OBJ_BEST_X=x.copy()
    D=float(np.dot(a, gradF))
    g=a[idx]*(gradF[idx]-D)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(F), g
```
If n=1, idx empty, g empty. minimize with 0 vars? Avoid n=1.

`_a_from_log_x(x, idx, ref, n)` as above.

`_normalize_candidate(a)`:
```
def _normalize_candidate(a):
    if a is None: return None
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 0.01:  # same as evaluator; for internal use 1e-12 maybe
       return None
    return a/S
```
But for optimization candidates, sum may be <0.01 though can scale. If sum very small positive, score invariant; use `if S <= 1e-12 return None`. `score_unnorm`:
```
def score_unnorm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=_conv_full(a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m<0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*a.size*m/(S*S))
```
For normalized sum1, matches. For a sum not 1, exact.

`make_templates(n, rng)`: no lambda. Need ensure nonnegative. Use `np.clip(t, 1e-6, None)`. Normalize.
```
def _normalize_template(t):
   t=np.asarray(t,float); t=np.clip(t,0,1000); S=t.sum(); if S<=0: t=np.ones(n); S=n; return t/S
```
Can't have helper? Top-level allowed. We'll define `_norm_arr(a)` top-level for sum normalize (internal). Use for record? record uses score and normalizes. `_norm_arr`:
```
def _norm_arr(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=1e-12:
      return np.ones(max(1,a.size), dtype=float)/max(1,a.size)
   return a/S
```
Top-level.

Template generation:
```
def _make_templates(n, rng):
    out=[]
    if n<=1:
       out.append(np.ones(n) if n>0 else np.array([]))
       return out
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x2=x*x
    t=np.ones(n)
    out.append(t)
    t=0.5 + 0.4*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    out.append(t)
    t=0.3 + 0.6*np.cos(np.pi*x)**2
    out.append(t)
    t=0.3 + 0.7*(1.0 - (2*x -1.0)**2)
    out.append(t)
    t=0.2 + 0.8*(2*x -1.0)**2
    out.append(t)
    t=0.5 + 0.4*np.abs(2*x-1.0)
    out.append(t)
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       t = x**p + (1.0-x)**p + 0.1
       out.append(t)
    # random smooth
    for i in range(2):
       f1=1.0+3.0*rng.random()
       p1=2*np.pi*rng.random()
       a1=0.1+0.3*rng.random()
       f2=2.0+8.0*rng.random()
       p2=2*np.pi*rng.random()
       a2=0.05+0.2*rng.random()
       t=0.5 + a1*np.cos(2*np.pi*f1*x + p1) + a2*np.cos(2*np.pi*f2*x + p2)
       out.append(t)
    # normalize each
    out2=[]
    for t in out:
       tt=_norm_arr(t)
       if tt.size==n:
          out2.append(tt)
    return out2
```
No lambda.

`_resample_to_n(a,n)`:
```
def _resample_to_n(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   m=a.size
   if m==0: return None
   if m==n: a=a.copy()
   else:
      if m < 2: a=np.full(n, float(a[0]))
      else:
         xo=np.linspace(0,1,m); xn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
         a=np.interp(xn, xo, a)
   return _norm_arr(a)
```

`_symmetrize(a): a=_norm_arr(0.5*(a+a[::-1]))`
`_lowpass(a, keep=0.2)`:
```
def _lowpass(a, keep_frac=0.2):
   n=a.size; if n<2 return a
   L=_pow2(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   kmax=int(keep_frac*n)
   if kmax < A.size:
      A[kmax+1:] = 0.0
   b=np.fft.irfft(A,L)[:n]
   return _norm_arr(np.maximum(b,0.0))
```

`make_top_weights`:
```
def _make_top_weights(c, K=100, alpha=0.7, beta_smooth=0.0):
   L=int(c.size)
   if L <=0: return None
   cm=float(np.max(c))
   p_top=np.zeros(L)
   if K > 0:
      if K < L:
         idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
      else:
         idx=np.arange(L)
      p_top[idx]=1.0/float(idx.size)
   if alpha >= 1.0:
      p=p_top.copy()
   else:
      if beta_smooth <=0 or cm<=0:
         q=np.full(L, 1.0/L)
      else:
         scaled=beta_smooth*(c-cm)
         np.minimum(scaled,0.0,out=scaled)
         exps=np.exp(scaled); s=exps.sum()
         if s <=0:
            q=np.full(L,1.0/L)
         else:
            q=exps/s
      p=alpha*p_top + (1.0-alpha)*q
   s=p.sum()
   if s <=0:
      p=np.full(L,1.0/L)
      s=L
   return p/s
```
If K=0, p_top zeros; if alpha<1, okay. If alpha=1 and K=0, p zero -> uniform fallback.

`run_lbfgs_direct` and `_log`: Let's write both.

```
def _run_lbfgs_direct(...):
    global _OPT_N,...
    a0=_normalize_candidate(a0)
    if a0 is None: return (np.inf,None)
    n=a0.size
    if n < 2:
        return (score_unnorm(a0), a0)
    if p is not None:
       p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
       if p.size != 2*n-1: # fallback
           p=np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
       psum=p.sum()
       if psum<=0: p=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1)); psum=1
       else: p=p/psum
    _OPT_N=n; _OPT_MODE=mode; _OPT_BETA=float(beta if beta is not None else 1e5); _OPT_P=p
    _OBJ_BEST_VAL=np.inf; _OBJ_BEST_X=a0.copy()
    _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,float(run_time))
    x0=a0.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,10.0)]*n
    mom = 20 if n <= 10000 else 10
    opts={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxls':30,'iprint':-1,'m':mom}
    res=None
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_direct, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except _OptTimeout:
       res=None
    except Exception:
       res=None
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=score_unnorm(a0)
    # consider candidates
    if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
       cand=_normalize_candidate(res.x)
       if cand is not None:
          sc=score_unnorm(cand)
          if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=cand
    cand=_normalize_candidate(_OBJ_BEST_X)
    if cand is not None:
       sc=score_unnorm(cand)
       if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=cand
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Catch Exception to avoid crash? It may hide bugs but safe. If an exception from scipy due memory, return current. Good.

```
def _a_from_log_x(x, idx, ref, n):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    a=np.empty(n)
    if x.size:
       mx=float(np.max(x))
       if mx<0: mx=0
       a[idx]=np.exp(x-mx)
    else: mx=0
    if mx < 700: a[ref]=math.exp(-mx)
    else: a[ref]=0.0
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300:
       a.fill(1.0); a/=n
    else: a/=S
    return a
```

Recomputing prefix/suffix helper top-level `_prefix_suffix_max(c)`:
```
def _prefix_suffix_max(c):
   cc=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   L=cc.size
   pref=np.empty(L+1); pref[0]=0.0
   if L>0:
      acc=0.0
      # use np.maximum.accumulate: pref[1:]=np.maximum.accumulate(cc)
      pref[1:]=np.maximum.accumulate(cc)
   suff=np.empty(L+1); suff[L]=0.0
   if L>0:
      rev_acc=np.maximum.accumulate(cc[::-1])
      suff[:L]=rev_acc[::-1]
   return pref, suff
```
This allocates; fine.

def _mirror_lse(a0, beta=1e6, n_iters=500, max_time=120.0):
   a=_normalize_candidate(a0); if None return
   n=a.size
   start=time.monotonic(); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_unnorm(a)
   no_improve=0
   factors=(0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0)
   for it in range(int(n_iters)):
      if time.monotonic()-start > max_time: break
      c=_conv_full(a); cm=max(c); scaled=min(beta*(c-cm),0); exps...; out=h=4nout
      if h.max() <= 1e-300: break
      step_base=1.0/h.max()
      best_it_sc=best_sc? current_sc (score of a)
      current_sc = 2n*cm  # since sum=1? a normalized sum1, but mirror normalizes after; yes a sum1. Use score_unnorm? cm exact.
      # But after previous normalization, a sum1. Use current_sc=2*n*cm.
      best_a_it=a; best_sc_it=current_sc
      for fac in factors:
         eta=fac*step_base
         loga=np.log(a+1e-300) - eta*h
         loga -= loga.max()
         an=np.exp(loga); an/=an.sum()
         sc=score_unnorm(an)  # conv one
         if sc < best_sc_it:
             best_sc_it=sc; best_a_it=an
      if best_sc_it < current_sc - 1e-12:
         a=best_a_it; current_sc=best_sc_it; no_improve=0
         if current_sc < best_sc: best_sc=current_sc; best_a=a.copy()
      else:
         no_improve +=1
         if no_improve > 80: break
   return best_a
```
Using `score_unnorm` on candidate does conv each. For current a, we already have c; no need recompute. `score_unnorm(an)` uses FFT. Good. If beta high, h sparse. Could adapt beta each outer? In propose, call with multiple betas.

_GLOBAL_BEST_SC = np.inf

_global_run_start = 0.0

def record_candidate(a, label=""):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_SC, _GLOBAL_BEST_A
    c=_normalize_candidate(a)
    if c is None:
        return
    sc=score_unnorm(c)
    if sc < _GLOBAL_BEST_SC:
        _GLOBAL_BEST_SC=sc; _GLOBAL_BEST_A=c.copy()
        print(f"NEW BEST {sc:.9f} n={c.size} {label} t={time.monotonic()-_global_run_start:.0f}s", flush=True)
    # maintain good list
    if len(_GLOBAL_GOOD) < 8:
       _GLOBAL_GOOD.append((sc,c.copy()))
    else:
       # only replace if better than worst and different length? We'll replace worst if sc better.
       worst_idx=0; worst_val=_GLOBAL_GOOD[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(_GLOBAL_GOOD)):
           if _GLOBAL_GOOD[i][0] > worst_val:
               worst_val=_GLOBAL_GOOD[i][0]; worst_idx=i
       if sc < worst_val:
           _GLOBAL_GOOD[worst_idx]=(sc,c.copy())
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
over non-negative sequences a.

Main ideas:
- Normalize to sum=1 internally (objective is scale-invariant).
- Strictly use FFT for convolution and exact LSE/mean-peak gradients.
- Unconstrained L-BFGS-B in two reparameterizations:
    * unnormalized non-negative variables with exact scale-free gradient;
    * log-ratio variables for positive support and robustness to active zeros.
- Alternating active-peak (top-K) quadratic objectives with adaptive weights.
- Exact coordinate descent over one coordinate, with incremental convolution
  updates and full 1-D score evaluation on a small grid.
- Multiplicative LSE mirror descent for cheap polishing.
- Multiple initializations, symmetries, low-pass transforms, and a few other
  lengths to avoid getting stuck in a bad local basin.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class _OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


# -----------------------------
# Global optimizer state
# -----------------------------
_OPT_MODE = 0          # 0 = LSE, 1 = fixed weighted quadratic
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_IDX = None
_OPT_REF = -1
_OPT_BETA = 1.0
_OPT_P = None
_OPT_RUN_DEADLINE = 0.0

_OBJ_BEST_VAL = float("inf")
_OBJ_BEST_X = None

_GLOBAL_BEST_SC = float("inf")
_GLOBAL_BEST_A = None
_GLOBAL_GOOD = []
_global_run_start = 0.0


# -----------------------------
# Small numerical helpers
# -----------------------------
def _pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _norm_arr(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, returning a finite non-negative array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _normalize_candidate(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, or return None if invalid."""
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _conv_full(a):
    """Exact linear self-convolution via zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _crosscorr_p_a(a, p):
    """
    out_i = sum_j p_{i+j} a_j.
    This is 1/2 of the gradient of sum_k p_k c_k with respect to a_i
    when c = a * a.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(p[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ra = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    ra[:n] = a[::-1]

    pp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    m = min(int(p.size), L)
    pp[:m] = p[:m]

    out = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ra, L) * np.fft.rfft(pp, L), L)
    return out[1:n]


def _score_grad_norm(a, mode, beta, p):
    """
    Score and ambient gradient for the normalized a (sum(a)=1).

    mode=0: LSE smooth max of convolution.
    mode=1: fixed weighted quadratic sum_k p_k c_k.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if n == 1:
        x = float(a[0])
        c0 = x * x
        return float(2.0 * c0), np.array([4.0 * x], dtype=np.float64)

    c = _conv_full(a)

    if mode == 0:
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if beta is None or beta <= 0.0:
            psmooth = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
            Lval = cm
        else:
            scaled = float(beta) * (c - cm)
            np.minimum(scaled, 0.0, out=scaled)
            exps = np.exp(scaled)
            s = float(exps.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                psmooth = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
                Lval = cm
            else:
                psmooth = exps / s
                Lval = cm + math.log(s) / float(beta)
        p = psmooth

    out = _crosscorr_p_a(a, p)

    if mode == 0:
        F = 2.0 * n * float(Lval)
    else:
        if p is None:
            p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        P = float(np.dot(p, c))
        F = 2.0 * n * P

    gradF = 4.0 * n * out
    return float(F), gradF


def score_unnorm(a):
    """Exact objective for any non-negative vector a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


# -----------------------------
# Objective wrappers for scipy
# -----------------------------
def _obj_direct(x):
    """Unnormalized non-negative variable x, objective normalized internally."""
    global _OBJ_BEST_VAL, _OBJ_BEST_X

    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout

    n = int(_OPT_N)
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        if m > 0:
            b[:m] = a[:m]
        a = b

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        S = 1.0

    an = a / S
    F, gradF = _score_grad_norm(an, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P)

    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout

    if not math.isfinite(F):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if F < _OBJ_BEST_VAL:
        _OBJ_BEST_VAL = float(F)
        _OBJ_BEST_X = a.copy()

    D = float(np.dot(an, gradF))
    g = (gradF - D) / S
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(F), g


def _a_from_log_x(x, idx, ref, n):
    """Reconstruct a from log-ratio variables with fixed reference index."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if x.size:
        mx = float(np.max(x))
        if mx < 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
        a[idx] = np.exp(x - mx)
    else:
        mx = 0.0

    if mx < 700.0:
        a[ref] = math.exp(-mx)
    else:
        a[ref] = 0.0

    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0)
        a /= float(n)
    else:
        a /= S
    return a


def _obj_log(x):
    """Log-ratio variables x_i = log(a_i / a_ref)."""
    global _OBJ_BEST_VAL, _OBJ_BEST_X

    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout

    n = int(_OPT_N)
    idx = _OPT_IDX
    ref = int(_OPT_REF)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size:
        mx = float(np.max(x))
        if mx < 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
    else:
        mx = 0.0

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size:
        a[idx] = np.exp(x - mx)
    if mx < 700.0:
        a[ref] = math.exp(-mx)
    else:
        a[ref] = 0.0

    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0)
        a /= float(n)
        S = 1.0
    else:
        a /= S

    F, gradF = _score_grad_norm(a, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P)

    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout

    if not math.isfinite(F):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    if F < _OBJ_BEST_VAL:
        _OBJ_BEST_VAL = float(F)
        _OBJ_BEST_X = x.copy()

    D = float(np.dot(a, gradF))
    g = a[idx] * (gradF[idx] - D)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(F), g


# -----------------------------
# L-BFGS runners
# -----------------------------
def _run_lbfgs_direct(a0, mode=0, beta=None, p=None,
                      maxiter=1000, maxfun=8000, run_time=180.0):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P
    global _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE, _OBJ_BEST_VAL, _OBJ_BEST_X

    a0 = _normalize_candidate(a0)
    if a0 is None:
        return float("inf"), None

    n = int(a0.size)
    if n < 2:
        return score_unnorm(a0), a0

    if p is not None:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != 2 * n - 1:
            p = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1))
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 1e-300:
            p = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1))
        else:
            p = p / ps
    else:
        p = None

    _OPT_N = n
    _OPT_MODE = int(mode)
    _OPT_BETA = float(beta if beta is not None else 1.0)
    _OPT_P = p
    _OBJ_BEST_VAL = float("inf")

    # Start with sum ~= 10 to keep components away from the zero bound.
    a0 = a0.copy()
    s0 = float(a0.sum())
    if s0 > 1e-300:
        a0 *= (10.0 / s0)
    _OBJ_BEST_X = a0.copy()

    _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(run_time))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    mom = 20 if n <= 10000 else 10
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
        "iprint": -1,
        "m": mom,
    }

    res = None
    try:
        res = minimize(_obj_direct, a0, method="L-BFGS-B",
                       jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except _OptTimeout:
        res = None
    except Exception:
        res = None

    best_a = a0 / float(a0.sum())
    best_sc = score_unnorm(best_a)

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        cand = _normalize_candidate(res.x)
        if cand is not None:
            sc = score_unnorm(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand

    cand = _normalize_candidate(_OBJ_BEST_X)
    if cand is not None:
        sc = score_unnorm(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def _run_lbfgs_log(a0, mode=0, beta=None, p=None,
                   maxiter=1000, maxfun=8000, run_time=180.0,
                   lb=-50.0, ub=50.0, ref=None):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_IDX, _OPT_REF, _OPT_MODE, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_P
    global _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE, _OBJ_BEST_VAL, _OBJ_BEST_X

    a0 = _normalize_candidate(a0)
    if a0 is None:
        return float("inf"), None

    n = int(a0.size)
    if n < 2:
        return score_unnorm(a0), a0

    if p is not None:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != 2 * n - 1:
            p = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1))
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 1e-300:
            p = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1))
        else:
            p = p / ps
    else:
        p = None

    if ref is None:
        ref = int(np.argmax(a0))
    ref = int(ref)
    if ref < 0 or ref >= n:
        ref = 0

    idx_list = []
    for i in range(n):
        if i != ref:
            idx_list.append(i)
    idx = np.asarray(idx_list, dtype=np.int64)

    _OPT_N = n
    _OPT_IDX = idx
    _OPT_REF = ref
    _OPT_MODE = int(mode)
    _OPT_BETA = float(beta if beta is not None else 1.0)
    _OPT_P = p

    ar = float(a0[ref])
    if ar < 1e-300:
        ar = 1e-300

    x0 = np.empty(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for pos, ii in enumerate(idx):
        v = float(a0[ii]) / ar
        if v < 1e-300:
            v = 1e-300
        x0[pos] = math.log(v)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, lb, ub)

    _OBJ_BEST_VAL = float("inf")
    _OBJ_BEST_X = x0.copy()
    _OPT_RUN_DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(run_time))

    bounds = [(lb, ub)] * max(0, n - 1)
    mom = 20 if n <= 10000 else 10
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
        "iprint": -1,
        "m": mom,
    }

    res = None
    try:
        res = minimize(_obj_log, x0, method="L-BFGS-B",
                       jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except _OptTimeout:
        res = None
    except Exception:
        res = None

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = score_unnorm(best_a)

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        cand = _a_from_log_x(res.x, idx, ref, n)
        cand = _normalize_candidate(cand)
        if cand is not None:
            sc = score_unnorm(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand

    cand = _a_from_log_x(_OBJ_BEST_X, idx, ref, n)
    cand = _normalize_candidate(cand)
    if cand is not None:
        sc = score_unnorm(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand

    return float(best_sc), best_a


# -----------------------------
# Weighted active-peak helpers
# -----------------------------
def _make_top_weights(c, K=100, alpha=0.7, beta_smooth=0.0):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return None

    cm = float(np.max(c))

    p_top = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if K > 0:
        if K < L:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L - int(K))[-int(K):]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        p_top[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)

    if alpha >= 1.0:
        p = p_top.copy()
    else:
        if beta_smooth <= 0.0 or cm <= 0.0:
            q = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            scaled = float(beta_smooth) * (c - cm)
            np.minimum(scaled, 0.0, out=scaled)
            exps = np.exp(scaled)
            s = float(exps.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                q = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                q = exps / s
        p = float(alpha) * p_top + (1.0 - float(alpha)) * q

    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(L)
    return p / s


# -----------------------------
# Other local methods
# -----------------------------
def _prefix_suffix_max(c):
    cc = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    L = int(cc.size)
    pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    suff = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    suff[L] = 0.0
    if L > 0:
        pref[1:] = np.maximum.accumulate(cc)
        rev_acc = np.maximum.accumulate(cc[::-1])
        suff[:L] = rev_acc[::-1]
    return pref, suff


def _coordinate_peak_descent(a0, n_sweeps=25, max_coords=200, n_peaks=5,
                             factors=(0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.25, 1.7, 2.3),
                             max_time=300.0, start_time=None):
    a = _normalize_candidate(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None

    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2:
        return a

    if start_time is None:
        start_time = time.monotonic()
    max_time = float(max_time)

    c = _conv_full(a)
    S = 1.0
    current_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    for _sweep in range(int(n_sweeps)):
        if time.monotonic() - start_time > max_time:
            break

        c = _conv_full(a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-12:
            break

        current_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c))) / (S * S)
        if current_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = current_sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if S < 0.35 or S > 2.8:
            a = a / S
            c = _conv_full(a)
            S = 1.0
            current_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
            if current_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = current_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            continue

        two_a = 2.0 * a
        Lc = int(c.size)
        pref, suff = _prefix_suffix_max(c)
        cpos = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        if int(n_peaks) < Lc:
            peak_idx = np.argpartition(cpos, Lc - int(n_peaks))[-int(n_peaks):]
        else:
            peak_idx = np.arange(Lc)
        if peak_idx.size > 1:
            peak_idx = peak_idx[np.argsort(cpos[peak_idx])[::-1]]

        entries = []
        seen = set()
        top_local = 50

        for kk in peak_idx:
            k = int(kk)
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(k, n - 1)
            if je < js:
                continue
            start = k - je
            stop = k - js
            if stop < start:
                continue
            seg = a[start:stop + 1]
            vals = 2.0 * seg[::-1]

            if vals.size > top_local:
                loc = np.argpartition(vals, vals.size - top_local)[-top_local:]
            else:
                loc = np.arange(vals.size)

            for li in loc:
                gj = js + int(li)
                if gj in seen or gj < 0 or gj >= n:
                    continue
                if float(a[gj]) <= 0.0:
                    continue
                seen.add(gj)
                entries.append((float(vals[li]), gj))

        # Add a few large coordinates, which can help in rescaling.
        if n > 50:
            topn = 30
            if topn < n:
                topa = np.argpartition(a, n - topn)[-topn:]
            else:
                topa = np.arange(n)
            for ti in topa:
                gj = int(ti)
                if gj in seen or float(a[gj]) <= 0.0:
                    continue
                seen.add(gj)
                entries.append((float(a[gj]), gj))

        entries.sort(reverse=True)

        accepted = 0
        for entry in entries[:int(max_coords)]:
            if time.monotonic() - start_time > max_time:
                break
            contrib, j = entry
            j = int(j)
            aj = float(a[j])
            if aj <= 0.0:
                continue

            unaff = max(float(pref[j]), float(suff[j + n]))
            cseg = c[j:j + n]

            best_delta = 0.0
            best_sc_j = current_sc

            for fac in factors:
                fac = float(fac)
                if fac <= 0.0 and aj <= 0.0:
                    continue
                x = aj * fac
                delta = x - aj
                if delta == 0.0:
                    continue
                Snew = S + delta
                if Snew <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                temp = cseg + (two_a * delta)
                if j < n:
                    temp[j] += delta * delta
                mseg = float(np.max(temp)) if temp.size else 0.0
                if mseg < 0.0:
                    mseg = 0.0
                mnew = mseg if mseg > unaff else unaff
                if mnew < 0.0:
                    mnew = 0.0

                scj = 2.0 * n * mnew / (Snew * Snew)
                if math.isfinite(scj) and scj < best_sc_j:
                    best_sc_j = scj
                    best_delta = delta

            if best_delta != 0.0 and best_sc_j < current_sc - 1e-13:
                c[j:j + n] += two_a * best_delta
                c[2 * j] += best_delta * best_delta
                a[j] += best_delta
                if a[j] < 0.0:
                    a[j] = 0.0
                two_a[j] = 2.0 * float(a[j])
                S += best_delta
                current_sc = best_sc_j

                if current_sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = current_sc
                    best_a = a.copy()

                accepted += 1
                if accepted % 8 == 0:
                    pref, suff = _prefix_suffix_max(c)

        if S < 0.35 or S > 2.8:
            a = a / S
            c = _conv_full(a)
            S = 1.0
            current_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
            if current_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = current_sc
                best_a = a.copy()

    return _normalize_candidate(best_a)


def _mirror_lse(a0, beta=1e6, n_iters=1500, max_time=240.0):
    a = _normalize_candidate(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None

    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2:
        return a

    start = time.monotonic()
    max_time = float(max_time)

    c = _conv_full(a)
    cur_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc
    no_improve = 0
    stages = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0)

    for _it in range(int(n_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - start > max_time:
            break

        c = _conv_full(a)
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if cm <= 0.0:
            break

        scaled = float(beta) * (c - cm)
        np.minimum(scaled, 0.0, out=scaled)
        exps = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(exps.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = exps / s

        out = _crosscorr_p_a(a, p)
        h = 4.0 * n * out
        hm = float(np.max(h))
        if hm <= 1e-300:
            break

        base = 1.0 / hm
        local_best_sc = cur_sc
        local_best_a = a

        for st in stages:
            eta = float(st) * base
            loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - eta * h
            mx = float(np.max(loga))
            loga -= mx
            an = np.exp(loga)
            an = _normalize_candidate(an)
            if an is None:
                continue
            sc = score_unnorm(an)
            if sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best_a = an

        if local_best_sc < cur_sc - 1e-13:
            a = local_best_a
            cur_sc = local_best_sc
            no_improve = 0
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 120:
                break

    return _normalize_candidate(best_a)


# -----------------------------
# Initializations and transforms
# -----------------------------
def _resample_to_n(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    if m == n:
        a = a.copy()
    elif m == 1:
        a = np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    return _norm_arr(a)


def _symmetrize(a):
    a = _norm_arr(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        return a
    return _norm_arr(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))


def _lowpass(a, keep_frac=0.2):
    a = _norm_arr(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        return a
    n = int(a.size)
    L = _pow2(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    kmax = int(keep_frac * n)
    if kmax + 1 < A.size:
        A[kmax + 1:] = 0.0
    b = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:n]
    return _norm_arr(np.maximum(b, 0.0))


def _make_templates(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return [np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)]

    out = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out.append(t)

    t = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    out.append(t)

    t = 0.3 + 0.6 * np.cos(np.pi * x) ** 2
    out.append(t)

    t = 0.3 + 0.7 * (1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    out.append(t)

    t = 0.2 + 0.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
    out.append(t)

    t = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)
    out.append(t)

    for powr in (0.7, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4):
        t = x ** powr + (1.0 - x) ** powr + 0.1
        out.append(t)

    for _r in range(3):
        f1 = 1.0 + 3.0 * float(rng.random())
        p1 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        a1 = 0.08 + 0.25 * float(rng.random())
        f2 = 2.0 + 8.0 * float(rng.random())
        p2 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        a2 = 0.03 + 0.18 * float(rng.random())
        t = 0.5 + a1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + p1) + a2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + p2)
        out.append(t)

    clean = []
    for t in out:
        tt = _norm_arr(t)
        if tt is not None and tt.size == n:
            clean.append(tt)
    return clean


# -----------------------------
# Global best tracking
# -----------------------------
def record_candidate(a, label=""):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_SC, _GLOBAL_BEST_A, _GLOBAL_GOOD
    c = _normalize_candidate(a)
    if c is None:
        return
    sc = score_unnorm(c)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return

    if sc < _GLOBAL_BEST_SC:
        _GLOBAL_BEST_SC = float(sc)
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A = c.copy()
        try:
            print("NEW BEST {:.9f} n={} label={} t={:.0f}s".format(
                sc, c.size, label, time.monotonic() - _global_run_start), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            print("NEW BEST", sc, "n=", c.size, label, flush=True)

    if len(_GLOBAL_GOOD) < 10:
        _GLOBAL_GOOD.append((float(sc), c.copy()))
    else:
        worst_i = 0
        worst_v = _GLOBAL_GOOD[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(_GLOBAL_GOOD)):
            if _GLOBAL_GOOD[i][0] > worst_v:
                worst_v = _GLOBAL_GOOD[i][0]
                worst_i = i
        if sc < worst_v:
            _GLOBAL_GOOD[worst_i] = (float(sc), c.copy())


# -----------------------------
# Main entry point
# -----------------------------
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_SC, _GLOBAL_BEST_A, _GLOBAL_GOOD, _global_run_start

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    _global_run_start = t0
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    work_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    _GLOBAL_BEST_SC = float("inf")
    _GLOBAL_BEST_A = None
    _GLOBAL_GOOD.clear()

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 16:
                parent = _normalize_candidate(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        record_candidate(parent, "parent")
    else:
        n0 = 6962
        best_t = None
        best_t_sc = float("inf")
        tt_list = _make_templates(n0, rng)
        for tt in tt_list:
            sc = score_unnorm(tt)
            if sc < best_t_sc:
                best_t_sc = sc
                best_t = tt
        if best_t is not None:
            record_candidate(best_t, "init-template")

    if _GLOBAL_BEST_A is None:
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A = _norm_arr(np.ones(6962))
        record_candidate(_GLOBAL_BEST_A, "fallback")

    main_a = _normalize_candidate(_GLOBAL_BEST_A).copy()
    main_n = int(main_a.size)
    main_sc = score_unnorm(main_a)

    print("Start: best={:.9f} n={}".format(_GLOBAL_BEST_SC, main_n), flush=True)

    # Quick same-length transforms.
    sym = _symmetrize(main_a)
    if sym is not None:
        sc = score_unnorm(sym)
        if sc < main_sc:
            main_a = sym
            main_sc = sc
        record_candidate(sym, "main-sym")

    lp1 = _lowpass(main_a, 0.08)
    if lp1 is not None:
        sc = score_unnorm(lp1)
        if sc < main_sc:
            main_a = lp1
            main_sc = sc
        record_candidate(lp1, "main-lowpass-0.08")

    lp2 = _lowpass(main_a, 0.20)
    if lp2 is not None:
        sc = score_unnorm(lp2)
        if sc < main_sc:
            main_a = lp2
            main_sc = sc
        record_candidate(lp2, "main-lowpass-0.20")

    # -----------------------
    # Phase 1: LSE L-BFGS on main length
    # -----------------------
    lse_mults = (20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0)
    for mult in lse_mults:
        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 150.0:
            break
        c = _conv_full(main_a)
        cmax = max(1.0 / float(max(1, main_a.size)), float(np.max(c)))
        beta = float(mult) / cmax
        run_t = min(300.0, rem - 60.0)
        sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_direct(main_a, mode=0, beta=beta,
                                   maxiter=2500, maxfun=20000,
                                   run_time=run_t)
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            if sc < main_sc - 1e-13:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "LSE-direct-beta={:.3e}".format(beta))

        if rem < 150.0:
            break
        run_t = min(180.0, rem - 60.0)
        sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_log(main_a, mode=0, beta=beta,
                                maxiter=1500, maxfun=10000,
                                run_time=run_t)
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            if sc < main_sc - 1e-13:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "LSE-log-beta={:.3e}".format(beta))

    # -----------------------
    # Phase 2: Active top-K weighted objectives
    # -----------------------
    top_settings = (
        (30, 0.55, 35.0),
        (80, 0.65, 45.0),
        (200, 0.75, 60.0),
        (500, 0.85, 80.0),
    )
    for K, alpha, smooth_mult in top_settings:
        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 130.0:
            break
        c = _conv_full(main_a)
        cmax = max(1.0 / float(max(1, main_a.size)), float(np.max(c)))
        p = _make_top_weights(c, K=K, alpha=alpha,
                              beta_smooth=float(smooth_mult) / cmax)
        if p is None:
            continue
        run_t = min(180.0, rem - 60.0)
        sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_direct(main_a, mode=1, p=p,
                                   maxiter=1200, maxfun=10000,
                                   run_time=run_t)
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            if sc < main_sc - 1e-13:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "top-K-direct-K={}".format(K))

        if rem < 110.0:
            break
        run_t = min(120.0, rem - 60.0)
        sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_log(main_a, mode=1, p=p,
                                maxiter=800, maxfun=7000,
                                run_time=run_t)
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            if sc < main_sc - 1e-13:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "top-K-log-K={}".format(K))

    # -----------------------
    # Phase 3: Exact coordinate descent + mirror polish
    # -----------------------
    rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0:
        a2 = _coordinate_peak_descent(
            main_a, n_sweeps=25, max_coords=220, n_peaks=5, max_time=min(520.0, rem - 80.0)
        )
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            sc = score_unnorm(a2)
            if sc < main_sc:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "coord-main")

    rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        c = _conv_full(main_a)
        cmax = max(1.0 / float(max(1, main_a.size)), float(np.max(c)))
        for mult in (80.0, 400.0):
            rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 120.0:
                break
            a2 = _mirror_lse(main_a, beta=float(mult) / cmax,
                             n_iters=3000, max_time=min(260.0, rem - 60.0))
            if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
                sc = score_unnorm(a2)
                if sc < main_sc:
                    main_a = a2
                    main_sc = sc
                record_candidate(a2, "mirror-main-mult={}".format(mult))

    rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        a2 = _coordinate_peak_descent(
            main_a, n_sweeps=20, max_coords=180, n_peaks=5,
            max_time=min(360.0, rem - 60.0)
        )
        if a2 is not None and a2.size == main_n:
            sc = score_unnorm(a2)
            if sc < main_sc:
                main_a = a2
                main_sc = sc
            record_candidate(a2, "coord-main-2")

    # -----------------------
    # Phase 4: Explore a few other lengths
    # -----------------------
    alt_lengths = []
    for nn in (6962, 4096, 8192, 12288):
        if int(nn) != main_n and int(nn) not in alt_lengths:
            alt_lengths.append(int(nn))

    for nn in alt_lengths[:3]:
        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 320.0:
            break

        ar = _resample_to_n(_GLOBAL_BEST_A, nn)
        if ar is None:
            continue

        record_candidate(ar, "resample-{}".format(nn))

        c = _conv_full(ar)
        cmax = max(1.0 / float(nn), float(np.max(c)))

        if rem > 250.0:
            sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_direct(ar, mode=0, beta=(250.0 / cmax),
                                       maxiter=1000, maxfun=8000,
                                       run_time=min(160.0, rem - 120.0))
            record_candidate(a2, "LSE-alt-{}".format(nn))

        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 180.0:
            c = _conv_full(ar)
            p = _make_top_weights(c, K=120, alpha=0.75,
                                  beta_smooth=50.0 / max(float(np.max(c)), 1e-12))
            if p is not None:
                sc, a2 = _run_lbfgs_direct(ar, mode=1, p=p,
                                           maxiter=700, maxfun=6000,
                                           run_time=min(130.0, rem - 100.0))
                record_candidate(a2, "top-alt-{}".format(nn))

        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            a2 = _coordinate_peak_descent(ar, n_sweeps=12, max_coords=140,
                                          n_peaks=5, max_time=min(120.0, rem - 80.0))
            record_candidate(a2, "coord-alt-{}".format(nn))

        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            c = _conv_full(ar)
            cmax = max(1.0 / float(nn), float(np.max(c)))
            a2 = _mirror_lse(ar, beta=(300.0 / cmax),
                             n_iters=2000, max_time=min(90.0, rem - 70.0))
            record_candidate(a2, "mirror-alt-{}".format(nn))

    # -----------------------
    # Final polishing of global best
    # -----------------------
    if _GLOBAL_BEST_A is not None:
        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            a2 = _coordinate_peak_descent(
                _GLOBAL_BEST_A, n_sweeps=15, max_coords=180, n_peaks=5,
                max_time=min(300.0, rem - 70.0)
            )
            record_candidate(a2, "final-coord-global")

        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 100.0:
            c = _conv_full(_GLOBAL_BEST_A)
            cmax = max(1.0 / float(max(1, _GLOBAL_BEST_A.size)), float(np.max(c)))
            a2 = _mirror_lse(_GLOBAL_BEST_A, beta=(500.0 / cmax),
                             n_iters=2000, max_time=min(180.0, rem - 60.0))
            record_candidate(a2, "final-mirror-global")

        record_candidate(_symmetrize(_GLOBAL_BEST_A), "final-sym-global")
        record_candidate(_lowpass(_GLOBAL_BEST_A, 0.12), "final-lowpass-global")

        # Resample final back to common lengths; sometimes improves evaluation cost
        # and occasionally the discrete score as well.
        for nn in (6962, 8192, 4096):
            rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            ar = _resample_to_n(_GLOBAL_BEST_A, nn)
            if ar is not None:
                record_candidate(ar, "final-resample-{}".format(nn))

    # -----------------------
    # Final safe output
    # -----------------------
    out_a = _normalize_candidate(_GLOBAL_BEST_A)
    if out_a is None:
        out_a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    out_a = np.nan_to_num(out_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_a = np.clip(out_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(out_a.sum())
    if ss < 0.01:
        out_a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        ss = 64.0
    out_a = out_a / ss

    out = []
    for x in out_a:
        out.append(float(x))

    final_internal = score_unnorm(out_a)
    print("FINAL internal: {:.9f}, length={}".format(final_internal, len(out)), flush=True)

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print("Final verified: {:.9f}".format(verified), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
